<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:wght,FILL@100..700,0..1&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet"/><style>@layer base{html,body{margin:0;padding:0;}body{overscroll-behavior:none;}main>:first-child{margin-top:0!important;}main>:last-child{margin-bottom:0!important;}}::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none;}</style><script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script><script id="tailwind-config">tailwind.config = {

        "on-background": "#131b2e",

        "on-secondary": "#ffffff",

        "headline-lg-mobile": ["1.75rem", {"lineHeight": "2.25rem", "letterSpacing": "-0.01em", "fontWeight": "700"}],

};</script></head><body class="bg-background font-body-md text-on-surface antialiased"><header class="fixed top-0 inset-x-0 h-16 bg-surface-container-lowest/90 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_1px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)] z-40"><div class="h-16 w-full px-gutter flex items-center justify-between gap-space-md"><div class="flex items-center gap-space-md min-w-max"><img alt="Brand logo. - Primary color: #f59e0b

py-space-lg"><div class="flex flex-col w-full gap-space-lg">
<!-- TOP CONTEXT / FILAMENT PAGE HEADER -->
<section class="flex flex-col gap-space-md bg-surface-container-lowest p-space-lg rounded-lg shadow-sm">
<div class="flex flex-col xl:flex-row xl:items-center justify-between gap-space-md">
<!-- Identity & Subtitle -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm flex-wrap">
<h1 class="font-headline-md text-headline-md text-on-surface font-extrabold tracking-tight">
            لوحة متابعة الطفل: سارة عبد الرحمن
          </h1>
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-primary-fixed text-on-primary-fixed-variant font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-base text-primary-container" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">star</span>
            المستوى الذهبي
          </span>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">
<span class="flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold text-on-surface">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-base">school</span>
            مدرسة الأمل النموذجية
          </span>
<span class="text-outline-variant">•</span>
<span>الصف الأول الابتدائي (شعبة أ)</span>
<span class="text-outline-variant">•</span>
<span>الفصل الدراسي الثاني 1445-1446هـ</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Action Pill & Switcher -->
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-space-sm">
<!-- Switch Child Dropdown Button -->
<div class="relative group">
<button class="flex items-center gap-2.5 bg-surface-container-low hover:bg-surface-container text-on-surface px-4 py-2 rounded-full font-label-lg text-label-lg transition-all shadow-sm" type="button">
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-secondary"></span>
<span>سارة (الصف الأول)</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-on-surface-variant text-lg">arrow_drop_down</span>
</button>
<!-- Mock Dropdown List -->
<div class="absolute left-0 mt-2 w-52 bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-xl shadow-lg p-1.5 hidden group-hover:block z-50">
<div class="px-3 py-2 text-label-sm font-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-bold">التبديل بين الأبناء</div>
<a class="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 rounded bg-surface-container-low font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-bold" href="#">
<span>سارة (الصف الأول)</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-sm">check_circle</span>
</a>
<a class="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 rounded hover:bg-surface-container font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" href="#">
<span>عمر (مرحلة الروضة)</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-outline-variant text-sm">face_3</span>
</a>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Filament Actions -->
<button class="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-primary-container hover:brightness-105 active:translate-y-0.5 text-on-primary-container px-4 py-2 rounded-full font-label-lg text-label-lg font-bold shadow-sm transition-all" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-lg">picture_as_pdf</span>
<span>تصدير تقرير أسبوعي PDF</span>
</button>
<button class="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-surface-container-lowest hover:bg-surface-container-low text-secondary px-3.5 py-2 rounded-full font-label-lg text-label-lg font-bold shadow-sm transition-colors" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-lg">table_chart</span>
<span>تقرير درجات CSV</span>
</button>
<button class="flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-surface-container-low hover:bg-surface-container text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" title="تخصيص الواجهة" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-xl">tune</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Sync status metadata bar -->
<div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-space-xs text-label-sm font-label-sm text-on-surface-variant border-none">
<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
<span class="inline-block w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-secondary-fixed-dim animate-pulse"></span>
<span>آخر مزامنة مع منصة المدرسة: اليوم، 11:30 ص</span>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
<span class="text-secondary font-bold">النظام متصل ومحدّث لحظياً</span>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<!-- KPI STATS OVERVIEW (FILAMENT 4-GRID) -->
<section class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-space-md">
<!-- Card 1: Total XP -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-lg p-space-md flex flex-col justify-between shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant font-semibold">إجمالي النقاط والخبرة (Total XP)</span>
<div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-primary-fixed flex items-center justify-center text-primary font-bold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-xl" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">military_tech</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="mt-space-md flex flex-col gap-1">
<span class="font-headline-lg text-headline-lg text-on-surface font-extrabold tracking-tight">3,840 <span class="font-label-lg text-label-lg font-semibold text-primary">XP</span></span>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
<span class="inline-flex items-center text-secondary font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-sm">trending_up</span>
            +420 XP
          </span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">مكتسبة هذا الأسبوع</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Decorative Pill Accent -->
<div class="absolute -bottom-6 -left-6 w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-primary-fixed/20 pointer-events-none"></div>
</div>
<!-- Card 2: Accuracy Rate -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-lg p-space-md flex flex-col justify-between shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant font-semibold">نسبة دقة الإجابات (Accuracy)</span>
<div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-secondary-fixed flex items-center justify-center text-secondary font-bold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-xl">verified</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="mt-space-md flex flex-col gap-1">
<div class="flex items-baseline gap-2">
<span class="font-headline-lg text-headline-lg text-on-surface font-extrabold">94.2%</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-container font-bold">+2.8%</span>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
<div class="w-full bg-surface-container-high h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
<div class="bg-secondary h-2 rounded-full" style="width: 94.2%"></div>
</div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant whitespace-nowrap">تقييم صوتي</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Card 3: Daily Streak -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-lg p-space-md flex flex-col justify-between shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant font-semibold">سلسلة المواظبة (Daily Streak)</span>
<div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-error-container flex items-center justify-center text-error font-bold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-xl" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">local_fire_department</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="mt-space-md flex flex-col gap-1">
<span class="font-headline-lg text-headline-lg text-on-surface font-extrabold tracking-tight">14 <span class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant font-semibold">يوماً متتالياً</span></span>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-label-sm text-label-sm text-error font-bold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-sm">workspace_premium</span>
<span>شعلة نشطة • بطل الأسبوع بالفصل</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Card 4: Completed Lessons -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-lg p-space-md flex flex-col justify-between shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant font-semibold">التحديات والدروس المكتملة</span>
<div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-tertiary-fixed flex items-center justify-center text-tertiary font-bold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-xl">auto_stories</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="mt-space-md flex flex-col gap-1">
<span class="font-headline-lg text-headline-lg text-on-surface font-extrabold tracking-tight">28 <span class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">/ 30 درساً</span></span>
<div class="flex items-center justify-between font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">
<span class="font-semibold text-tertiary">نسبة إنجاز 93%</span>
<span>متبقٍ درسان فقط</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<!-- ASYMMETRIC MAIN GRID (8/12 - 4/12) -->
<section class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-space-lg items-start">
<!-- RIGHT MAIN COLUMN (8 COLS RTL) -->
<div class="lg:col-span-8 flex flex-col gap-space-lg">
<!-- Mastery Analytics Chart Card -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-lg p-space-lg shadow-sm flex flex-col gap-space-md">
<!-- Header with Tabs -->
<div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-space-sm pb-space-sm">
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
<h2 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold">
              مؤشر إتقان المهارات والمواد الدراسية (Mastery Analytics)
            </h2>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">
              تحليل أدائي دقيق مدعوم بنموذج الذكاء الاصطناعي لكل مجال تعليمي
            </p>
</div>
<!-- Chart Range Tabs -->
<div class="flex items-center bg-surface-container-low p-1 rounded-full self-start">
<button class="px-3.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary-container font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold shadow-sm transition-all" type="button">
              آخر 7 أيام
            </button>

class="relative pt-16 min-h-screen bg-surface px-space-lg py-space-lg w-full"><div class="flex flex-col w-full">
<div class="flex flex-col gap-space-lg w-full max-w-[1400px] mx-auto">
<!-- Breadcrumb & Top Bar -->

<div class="flex flex-col gap-1">

</div>
</div>
<!-- Metric Stats Ribbon (Filament KPI Widgets) -->

<!-- Metric 1 -->

<div class="flex items-start justify-between">

</div>
</div>
<div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-secondary-container/50 text-secondary flex items-center justify-center">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[26px]">family_restroom</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="mt-space-sm flex items-center gap-1 text-secondary font-label-sm text-label-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">check_circle</span>
<span>تم التحقق من الحسابات الرسمية بالكامل</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Metric 2 -->
<div class="relative overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-surface-container-lowest p-space-lg shadow-sm">
<div class="absolute -left-6 -bottom-6 w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-primary-fixed/30 pointer-events-none"></div>
<div class="flex items-start justify-between">
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant">إجمالي النقاط المشتركة</span>
<div class="flex items-baseline gap-2 mt-1">
<span class="font-headline-xl text-headline-xl text-primary font-extrabold">6,420</span>
<span class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-on-surface-variant font-semibold">XP مجمّعة</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-primary-fixed text-primary flex items-center justify-center">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[26px]">stars</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="mt-space-sm flex items-center gap-1 text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary-container text-[16px]">trending_up</span>
<span>+340 XP خلال الأسبوع الحالي</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Metric 3 -->
<div class="relative overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-surface-container-lowest p-space-lg shadow-sm">

<!-- Modal Container -->

<div class="p-space-lg flex flex-col gap-space-md">
<!-- Header -->
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-xs">
<div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-primary-fixed text-on-primary-fixed flex items-center justify-center font-bold">

<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">close</span>
</button>
</div>

<form class="flex flex-col gap-space-md mt-space-sm" onsubmit="event.preventDefault();">
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<label class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold text-on-surface">اسم الطفل الكامل (الرباعي)</label>
<input class="h-12 px-4 rounded-xl bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md text-body-md focus:outline-none focus:bg-surface-container-lowest" placeholder="مثال: يوسف عبد الرحمن آل إبراهيم" type="text"/>
</div>
<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-space-sm">

<option>الصف الثاني الابتدائي</option>
<option>الصف الثالث الابتدائي</option>
<option>الصف الرابع الابتدائي</option>
<option>الصف الخامس الابتدائي</option>
<option>الصف السادس الابتدائي</option>
</select>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<label class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold text-on-surface">الشعبة</label>
<input class="h-12 px-4 rounded-xl bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md text-body-md focus:outline-none focus:bg-surface-container-lowest" placeholder="مثال: أ أو ب" type="text"/>
</div>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<label class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold text-on-surface">رمز الربط المدرسي (Student ID / Noor Code)</label>
<div class="relative">
<input class="h-12 w-full px-4 rounded-xl bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md text-body-md focus:outline-none focus:bg-surface-container-lowest" placeholder="أدخل رمز التحقق المكون من 8 أرقام" type="text"/>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined absolute left-3 top-3 text-on-surface-variant">qr_code</span>
</div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">تحصل على هذا الرمز من إشعار المدرسة أو تطبيق نور</span>
</div>
<div class="p-space-md rounded-xl bg-secondary-container/20 text-on-secondary-container flex items-start gap-space-xs">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px] text-secondary shrink-0">info</span>
<div class="flex flex-col text-xs leading-relaxed">
<span class="font-bold">المزامنة التلقائية للمواد</span>
<span>بمجرد التحقق من الرمز، سيتم جلب كتب المناهج، الواجبات، ولوحة المعلمين تلقائياً خلال دقيقة واحدة.</span>
</div>
</div>
</form>
</div>
<!-- Modal Footer -->
<div class="p-space-lg bg-surface-container-low flex items-center justify-end gap-space-sm">
<button class="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-surface-container-lowest hover:bg-surface-container text-on-surface font-label-md text-label-md font-semibold transition-colors" id="cancelModalBtn" type="button">
          إلغاء
        </button>
<button class="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary-container font-label-md text-label-md font-bold shadow-[0_3px_0_#d97706] hover:translate-y-[1px] transition-transform" id="saveChildBtn" type="button">
          تأكيد التسجيل والربط
        </button>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<script>
    (function() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('addChildModal');
      const openBtn = document.getElementById('openAddModalBtn');
      const enrollBtn = document.getElementById('enrollChildBtn');
      const closeBtn = document.getElementById('closeModalBtn');
      const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('cancelModalBtn');
      const backdrop = document.getElementById('modalBackdrop');
      const switchBtn = document.getElementById('switchChildBtn');
      const refreshBtn = document.getElementById('refreshSyncBtn');

      function openModal() {
        modal.classList.remove('hidden');
      }

      function closeModal() {
        modal.classList.add('hidden');
      }

      if (openBtn) openBtn.addEventListener('click', openModal);
      if (enrollBtn) enrollBtn.addEventListener('click', openModal);
      if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
      if (cancelBtn) cancelBtn.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
      if (backdrop) backdrop.addEventListener('click', closeModal);

      if (switchBtn) {
        switchBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
          const originalText = switchBtn.innerHTML;
          switchBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] animate-spin">refresh</span><span>جاري التبديل إلى عمر...</span>';
          setTimeout(() => {
            alert('تم تبديل الحساب النشط بنجاح إلى: عمر عبد الرحمن (الصف الثالث)');
            switchBtn.innerHTML = originalText;
          }, 600);
        });
      }

      if (refreshBtn) {
        refreshBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
          const icon = refreshBtn.querySelector('.material-symbols-outlined');
          if (icon) icon.classList.add('animate-spin');
          setTimeout(() => {
            if (icon) icon.classList.remove('animate-spin');
            alert('تمت المزامنة بنجاح مع مدرسة الأمل النموذجية!');
          }, 900);
        });
      }
    })();
  </script>
</div></main></div></body></html>

<!-- Page Title & Quick Actions Row -->

        </p>
</div>

<h2 class="font-headline-md text-headline-md text-on-surface font-extrabold mb-space-xs">
        لا توجد مواد دراسية مسجلة حالياً لـ سارة
      </h2>
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed">
        لم تقم بتعيين دروس لطفلتك بعد أو لم يتم إسناد واجبات جديدة من المعلمة المشرفة.
        اختر الآن من مكتبة المحطات الست والأنشطة التفاعلية المناسبة لصف طفلتك لتنطلق في رحلة التعلم الذكي المليئة بالمرح والجوائز!
      </p>
</div>
<!-- Action Buttons Row -->
<div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-space-md mt-space-lg w-full max-w-lg z-10">
<!-- Primary Amber Button with Bouncy Tactile Shadow -->
<button class="w-full sm:w-auto flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-space-xs px-space-lg py-3.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary-fixed font-label-lg text-label-lg font-bold shadow-[0_4px_0_#d97706] hover:translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-[0_2px_0_#d97706] active:translate-y-1 active:shadow-none transition-all" onclick="alert('جاري فتح مكتبة المحطات الست والدروس التفاعلية...');" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">rocket_launch</span>
<span>استكشاف مكتبة المحطات الست 📚</span>
</button>
<!-- Secondary Outline Button -->
<button class="w-full sm:w-auto inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-space-xs px-space-lg py-3.5 rounded-full bg-surface-container-lowest text-secondary font-label-lg text-label-lg font-bold shadow-sm hover:bg-secondary-container/20 active:scale-95 transition-all" onclick="alert('جاري الاتصال بنظام المدرسة ومزامنة الخطط الدراسية...');" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">sync</span>
<span>مزامنة المقررات من المدرسة 🔄</span>
</button>
</div>
<!-- Micro-stat Indicator -->
<div class="mt-space-lg pt-space-md flex items-center justify-center gap-space-lg text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm z-10 flex-wrap">
<span class="flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-secondary">verified_user</span>
<span>معتمد وفق المعايير الوزارية</span>
</span>
<span class="hidden sm:inline">•</span>
<span class="flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-primary">psychology_alt</span>
<span>تغذية راجعة ذكية فورية</span>
</span>
<span class="hidden sm:inline">•</span>
<span class="flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-tertiary">sports_esports</span>
<span>نظام ألعاب تفاعلية ومحطات</span>
</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Recommended Starter Tracks Section (مسارات مقترحة للبدء السريع) -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-space-md mb-space-xl">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-xs">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary-container text-[24px]">recommend</span>
<div>
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold">
            مسارات مقترحة للبدء السريع لطفلتك
          </h3>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">
            مبنية خصيصاً لتناسب مرحلة التهيئة للصف الأول الأساسي
          </p>
</div>
</div>
<a class="font-label-md text-label-md text-secondary font-bold hover:underline hidden sm:flex items-center gap-1" href="#">
<span>عرض كل المسارات (18)</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">chevron_left</span>
</a>
</div>
<!-- 3 Starter Cards Grid -->
<div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-space-md">
<!-- Card 1: Arabic Track -->
<div class="group bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-3xl p-space-lg shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all flex flex-col justify-between relative overflow-hidden">
<div class="absolute top-0 right-0 left-0 h-1.5 bg-secondary"></div>
<div>
<div class="flex items-start justify-between mb-space-sm">
<div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-secondary-container/40 text-on-secondary-container flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-lg shadow-inner">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[26px]">mic_external_on</span>
</div>
<span class="px-space-sm py-0.5 rounded-full text-label-sm font-label-sm bg-secondary-container/40 text-on-secondary-container font-semibold">
              اللغة العربية
            </span>
</div>
<h4 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold group-hover:text-secondary transition-colors mb-space-xs">
            مسار مخارج الحروف وقراءتها
          </h4>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mb-space-md leading-relaxed">
            محطة التقييم الصوتي الذكية لتدريب سارة على نطق الحروف بالحركات القصيرة والطويلة مع تصحيح النطق بالذكاء الاصطناعي.
          </p>
<!-- Module Meta Tags -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm text-label-sm font-label-sm text-on-surface-variant mb-space-md">
<span class="flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-secondary">timer</span>
<span>15 دقيقة / يومياً</span>
</span>
<span>•</span>
<span class="flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-primary">stars</span>
<span>120 نقطة خبرة</span>
</span>
</div>
</div>
<button class="w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-space-xs py-2.5 px-space-md rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-container font-label-md text-label-md font-bold hover:bg-secondary hover:text-on-secondary active:scale-95 transition-all mt-space-sm" onclick="this.innerText='تمت الإضافة بنجاح ✓'; this.classList.remove('bg-secondary-container'); this.classList.add('bg-secondary', 'text-on-secondary');" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">add_circle</span>
<span>إضافة إلى مواد سارة</span>
</button>
</div>
<!-- Card 2: Math Track -->
<div class="group bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-3xl p-space-lg shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all flex flex-col justify-between relative overflow-hidden">
<div class="absolute top-0 right-0 left-0 h-1.5 bg-primary-container"></div>
<div>
<div class="flex items-start justify-between mb-space-sm">
<div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-primary-fixed text-on-primary-fixed flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-lg shadow-inner">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[26px]">calculate</span>
</div>
<span class="px-space-sm py-0.5 rounded-full text-label-sm font-label-sm bg-primary-container/20 text-on-primary-container font-semibold">
              الرياضيات التفاعلية
            </span>
</div>
<h4 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold group-hover:text-primary transition-colors mb-space-xs">
            مسار الحساب بالصور والأشكال
          </h4>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mb-space-md leading-relaxed">
            محطة الألغاز البصرية لتعليم العد من 1 إلى 20 ومقارنة الأطوال والأحجام بأسلوب ألعاب المغامرات المحبب للأطفال.
          </p>
<!-- Module Meta Tags -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm text-label-sm font-label-sm text-on-surface-variant mb-space-md">
<span class="flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-secondary">timer</span>
<span>10 دقائق / يومياً</span>
</span>
<span>•</span>
<span class="flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-primary">military_tech</span>
<span>وسام الحساب الصغير</span>
</span>
</div>
</div>
<button class="w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-space-xs py-2.5 px-space-md rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary-fixed font-label-md text-label-md font-bold shadow-[0_3px_0_#d97706] hover:translate-y-0.5 active:translate-y-1 transition-all mt-space-sm" onclick="this.innerText='تمت الإضافة بنجاح ✓'; this.classList.add('opacity-90');" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">add_circle</span>
<span>إضافة إلى مواد سارة</span>
</button>
</div>
<!-- Card 3: Science Track -->
<div class="group bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-3xl p-space-lg shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all flex flex-col justify-between relative overflow-hidden">
<div class="absolute top-0 right-0 left-0 h-1.5 bg-tertiary"></div>
<div>
<div class="flex items-start justify-between mb-space-sm">
<div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-tertiary-fixed text-on-tertiary-fixed flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-lg shadow-inner">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[26px]">potted_plant</span>
</div>
<span class="px-space-sm py-0.5 rounded-full text-label-sm font-label-sm bg-tertiary-fixed text-on-tertiary-fixed font-semibold">
              العلوم والحياة
            </span>
</div>
<h4 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold group-hover:text-tertiary transition-colors mb-space-xs">
            استكشاف الكائنات وعالم النبات
          </h4>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mb-space-md leading-relaxed">
            محطة العالم الصغير: تجارب تفاعلية ثلاثية الأبعاد لمعرفة أجزاء النبتة ودورات حياة الحيوانات الأليفة في البيئة المحيطة.
          </p>
<!-- Module Meta Tags -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm text-label-sm font-label-sm text-on-surface-variant mb-space-md">
<span class="flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-secondary">timer</span>
<span>12 دقيقة / يومياً</span>
</span>
<span>•</span>

</span>
</div>
</div>
<button class="w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-space-xs py-2.5 px-space-md rounded-full bg-surface-container-high text-on-surface font-label-md text-label-md font-bold hover:bg-tertiary hover:text-on-tertiary active:scale-95 transition-all mt-space-sm" onclick="this.innerText='تمت الإضافة بنجاح ✓'; this.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high', 'text-on-surface'); this.classList.add('bg-tertiary', 'text-on-tertiary');" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">add_circle</span>
<span>إضافة إلى مواد سارة</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Educational Stations Overview (المحطات الست الذكية لسنابل) -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-3xl p-space-lg mb-space-lg flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-space-md">
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-md">
<div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-surface-container-lowest flex items-center justify-center text-primary shadow-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[28px]">hub</span>
</div>
<div>
<h4 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold">
          ما هي محطات سنابل الست؟
        </h4>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">
          نظام تعليمي متكامل ينقل الطفل من التهيئة المعرفية وحتى الإتقان التام عبر 6 محطات ذكية ومترابطة.
        </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm">
<button class="px-space-md py-2 rounded-full bg-surface-container-lowest hover:bg-surface text-on-surface font-label-md text-label-md font-bold shadow-sm transition-colors" type="button">
        دليل أولياء الأمور
      </button>
<button class="px-space-md py-2 rounded-full bg-secondary text-on-secondary font-label-md text-label-md font-bold shadow-sm hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity" type="button">
        شاهد جولة توضيحية
      </button>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<script>
  // Tab Switching Micro-interaction for Filament Segmented Control
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => {
        b.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface', 'shadow-sm');
        b.classList.add('text-on-surface-variant');
      });
      this.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface', 'shadow-sm');
      this.classList.remove('text-on-surface-variant');
    });
  });

  // Small playful interaction for Sanbal mascot
  const mascot = document.getElementById('mascotImg');
  if (mascot) {
    mascot.addEventListener('click', () => {
      mascot.classList.add('scale-110', 'rotate-3');
      setTimeout(() => {
        mascot.classList.remove('scale-110', 'rotate-3');
      }, 350);
    });
  }
</script></main></div></body></html>

        </span>

</div>

</div>
<!-- Wednesday (Today) -->

</div>

</div>

</div>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Interactive Slide-over / Modal for Adding New Goal (Vanilla JS Controlled) -->
<div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-inverse-surface/40 backdrop-blur-sm hidden transition-opacity" id="newGoalDialog">
<div class="w-full max-w-lg bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-2xl shadow-2xl p-space-lg mx-space-md flex flex-col gap-space-md animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-200">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-xs">
<span class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary-container flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm">🎯</span>
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface">إضافة هدف وتحدٍ أسبوعي جديد</h3>
</div>
<button class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-surface-container-low hover:bg-surface-container text-on-surface flex items-center justify-center transition-colors" id="closeNewGoalModal" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">close</span>
</button>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-space-sm">
<div>
<label class="block font-label-md text-label-md font-bold text-on-surface mb-1">المادة التعليمية أو المجال</label>
<select class="w-full h-12 px-space-md rounded-xl bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-secondary">
<option>اللغة العربية وقواعد الإملاء</option>
<option>الرياضيات والتفكير المنطقي</option>
<option>العلوم والاستكشاف الذكي</option>
<option>التربية الأخلاقية وتطوير العادات</option>
</select>
</div>
<div>
<label class="block font-label-md text-label-md font-bold text-on-surface mb-1">عنوان التحدي / النشاط</label>
<input class="w-full h-12 px-space-md rounded-xl bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-secondary placeholder:text-on-surface-variant/60" placeholder="مثال: حفظ جدول الضرب للعدد ٤ وقراءة سطرين" type="text"/>
</div>
<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-space-sm">
<div>
<label class="block font-label-md text-label-md font-bold text-on-surface mb-1">نقاط المكافأة (XP)</label>
<input class="w-full h-12 px-space-md rounded-xl bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-secondary" type="number" value="50"/>
</div>
<div>
<label class="block font-label-md text-label-md font-bold text-on-surface mb-1">تاريخ الاستحقاق</label>
<input class="w-full h-12 px-space-md rounded-xl bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-secondary" type="date"/>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<label class="block font-label-md text-label-md font-bold text-on-surface mb-1">رسالة تشجيعية تظهر للطفل</label>
<textarea class="w-full p-space-sm rounded-xl bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-secondary placeholder:text-on-surface-variant/60" placeholder="أنت بطل يا سارة، نحن فخورون باجتهادك!" rows="2"></textarea>
</div>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-space-sm pt-space-xs">
<button class="px-space-md py-2.5 rounded-full bg-surface-container-high text-on-surface font-label-lg text-label-lg font-semibold hover:bg-surface-container transition-colors" id="cancelGoalBtn" type="button">
          إلغاء
        </button>
<button class="px-space-lg py-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary-container font-label-lg text-label-lg font-bold shadow-md hover:shadow-lg transition-all active:translate-y-0.5" id="saveGoalBtn" type="button">
          حفظ وتفعيل التحدي 🚀
        </button>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Micro Inline Interaction Logic -->
<script>
    (function() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('newGoalDialog');
      const openBtn = document.getElementById('openNewGoalModal');
      const closeBtn = document.getElementById('closeNewGoalModal');
      const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('cancelGoalBtn');
      const saveBtn = document.getElementById('saveGoalBtn');

      function openModal() {
        if (modal) {
          modal.classList.remove('hidden');
          modal.classList.add('flex');
        }
      }

      function closeModal() {
        if (modal) {
          modal.classList.add('hidden');
          modal.classList.remove('flex');
        }
      }

      if (openBtn) openBtn.addEventListener('click', openModal);
      if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
      if (cancelBtn) cancelBtn.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
      if (saveBtn) {
        saveBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
          closeModal();
          // Optional subtle confirmation toast / alert
        });
      }

      // Close modal on click outside content
      if (modal) {
        modal.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
          if (e.target === modal) closeModal();
        });
      }
    })();
  </script>
</div></main></div></body></html>

<!-- أهداف وتحديات الأسبوع - سنابل IQ -->

<!-- Top Mascot Welcome Banner -->
<section class="relative overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-l from-primary-fixed via-primary-fixed-dim/40 to-surface-container-low p-space-md md:p-space-lg shadow-[0_12px_36px_-6px_rgba(245,158,11,0.22)]">
<!-- Ambient glowing backdrops -->
<div class="absolute -top-16 -right-16 w-52 h-52 bg-primary-container/20 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none"></div>
<div class="absolute -bottom-16 -left-16 w-52 h-52 bg-secondary-fixed/40 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none"></div>
<div class="relative z-10 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-space-md">
<!-- Mascot + Speech bubble -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-md flex-1">
<div class="relative shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center">
<div class="absolute inset-0 rounded-full bg-primary-container/30 blur-md animate-pulse"></div>
<img alt="الكتكوت سنبل الذكي المرشد التعليمي" class="relative z-10 w-24 h-24 md:w-32 md:h-32 object-contain drop-shadow-xl hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-300" src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/aida/AEtjO1XNNtqthZZvgsdIwx199-1bUswZlDQ0_WLOVra2n6gyG_wS6A2kxMn5JU2sZyxs0O-_KDf6hfINekUHr4VdY66UqBlg9w9wdFaqsTyumYoX8Ur2T1oEFVcNhtJ6-M1a2t4C7_krACw9VZ8-oDhKixNWCij3ngv2-tQRexTq0c_ZLgPVBLqSOQiB-2nowUi-UpN8wzCdfd0CwRhJKZLJNLq8vKWSysYG7C8aq2p9I8UjMDTQe-SU3cQ3A1Yx"/>
<span class="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 bg-surface-container-lowest px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-label-sm font-bold text-on-primary-fixed-variant shadow-md">
            سنبل معك 🐥
          </span>
</div>
<div class="space-y-space-xs">
<!-- Speech Balloon -->
<div class="relative bg-surface-container-lowest text-on-surface p-space-md rounded-2xl md:rounded-3xl shadow-[0_8px_24px_rgba(15,23,42,0.06)] border-2 border-primary-container/20">
<p class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm md:text-headline-md text-on-primary-fixed leading-tight">
              أهلاً بك يا بطلة سارة! 🌟 جاهزة لمغامرة اليوم الشيقة؟
            </p>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-1">
              أكملي محطة واحدة اليوم للمحافظة على شعلتك النشطة <span class="text-primary font-bold">🔥 (+50 نقطة XP)</span>
</p>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm pt-1">
<button class="inline-flex items-center gap-space-xs px-space-md py-1.5 rounded-full bg-secondary text-on-secondary hover:bg-on-secondary-container text-label-md font-bold shadow-[0_4px_12px_rgba(0,106,97,0.25)] active:scale-95 transition-all" id="audioPromptBtn" onclick="toggleAudioSpeech()">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-lg" id="audioIcon">volume_up</span>
<span id="audioText">🔊 استمع لرسالة سنبل</span>
</button>
<span class="text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant hidden sm:inline-flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-secondary animate-ping inline-block"></span>
              مستوى القراءة: ممتاز (المستوى 1)
            </span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Quick Stats Card Pill -->
<div class="w-full md:w-auto shrink-0 bg-surface-container-lowest/80 backdrop-blur-md p-space-md rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-around md:justify-end gap-space-md">
<div class="text-center px-2">
<div class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">التقدم اليومي</div>
<div class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-primary font-extrabold">65%</div>
<div class="w-16 h-2 bg-surface-container rounded-full overflow-hidden mt-1 mx-auto">
<div class="w-[65%] h-full bg-primary-container rounded-full"></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="h-10 w-px bg-outline-variant/30"></div>
<div class="text-center px-2">
<div class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">وسام الأسبوع</div>
<div class="font-label-lg text-label-lg font-bold text-secondary flex items-center justify-center gap-0.5">
<span>تاج القراءة</span> 👑
          </div>
<div class="text-label-sm text-on-secondary-container mt-0.5 font-semibold">باقي خطوة</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Main Grid: Adventure Map + Side Booster Quest Panel -->
<div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-space-lg items-start">
<!-- Winding Adventure Path (8 cols desktop) -->
<section class="lg:col-span-8 bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-xl p-space-md md:p-space-lg shadow-[0_8px_32px_-4px_rgba(15,23,42,0.05)] relative overflow-hidden">
<!-- Top Section Header -->
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-space-sm pb-space-md border-b border-surface-container-low mb-space-md">
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-xs">
<div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-primary-fixed flex items-center justify-center text-primary-container">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl">explore</span>
</div>
<div>
<h2 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-extrabold">خريطة المغامرة السحرية</h2>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">سر في الدرب، واجمع النجوم وافتح القلعة الكبرى!</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
<span class="px-space-sm py-1 rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-container text-label-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-base">grade</span>
            3 من 12 نجمة
          </span>

<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-xl">my_location</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Playful Adventure Map Meadow Substrate -->
<div class="relative py-space-lg px-2 md:px-space-md bg-gradient-to-b from-surface-container-low/60 via-surface-container-lowest to-secondary-container/20 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden min-h-[780px] flex flex-col justify-between items-center">
<!-- Decorative subtle SVG winding path line behind milestones -->
<svg class="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full pointer-events-none" fill="none" preserveaspectratio="none" viewbox="0 0 600 900">
<path d="M 300 100 Q 150 220 280 320 T 360 520 T 220 720 T 300 850" opacity="0.8" stroke="#FDE68A" stroke-dasharray="10 14" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="26"></path>
<path d="M 300 100 Q 150 220 280 320 T 360 520 T 220 720 T 300 850" opacity="0.4" stroke="#F59E0B" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="12"></path>

<circle cx="110" cy="190" fill="#E2E7FF" opacity="0.6" r="22"></circle>
<circle cx="500" cy="380" fill="#E2E7FF" opacity="0.7" r="35"></circle>
<circle cx="530" cy="390" fill="#E2E7FF" opacity="0.7" r="25"></circle>
<circle cx="90" cy="620" fill="#E2E7FF" opacity="0.5" r="28"></circle>
</svg>
<!-- Milestone 1: COMPLETED (Top-center shifted) -->
<div class="relative z-10 w-full max-w-sm my-3 flex flex-col items-center group">
<div class="relative flex items-center justify-center">
<div class="w-20 h-20 md:w-24 md:h-24 rounded-full bg-secondary flex items-center justify-center shadow-[0_8px_20px_rgba(0,106,97,0.35)] text-on-secondary cursor-pointer hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-4xl font-extrabold">check</span>
</div>
<!-- 3 Floating Stars -->
<div class="absolute -top-3 flex items-center gap-1 bg-surface-container-lowest px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full shadow-md">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary-container text-base">star</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary-container text-base">star</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary-container text-base">star</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="mt-2 text-center bg-surface-container-lowest/95 backdrop-blur-sm px-4 py-2 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-secondary/20">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-body-md md:text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-extrabold">المحطة 1: أصوات الحروف السحرية</h3>
<span class="inline-block text-label-sm font-bold text-secondary">مكتملة بإتقان 100% ⭐⭐⭐</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Milestone 2: ACTIVE CURRENT STATION (Glowing, Pulsing, Big Action) -->
<div class="relative z-20 w-full max-w-md my-4 flex flex-col items-center">
<!-- Sanbal Mini Avatar standing next to Current Station with pointer -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2 animate-bounce">
<div class="bg-primary-container text-on-primary font-bold text-label-md px-3 py-1 rounded-full shadow-md flex items-center gap-1">
<span>أنتِ هنا يا سارة!</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-base">arrow_downward</span>
</div>

</div>
<!-- Pulsing Radiant Interactive Stone -->
<div class="relative flex items-center justify-center">
<!-- Pulsing Rings -->
<div class="absolute -inset-4 rounded-full bg-primary-container/30 animate-ping opacity-75"></div>
<div class="absolute -inset-2 rounded-full bg-primary-fixed-dim/50 blur-md"></div>
<button class="relative z-10 w-28 h-28 md:w-32 md:h-32 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-b from-primary-container to-primary flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-on-primary shadow-[0_12px_32px_rgba(245,158,11,0.5)] border-4 border-surface-container-lowest hover:scale-105 active:scale-95 transition-all">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-4xl md:text-5xl">edit_note</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm font-extrabold text-on-primary mt-0.5">المحطة الحالية</span>
</button>
</div>
<!-- Station Card Details -->
<div class="mt-3 w-full bg-surface-container-lowest p-space-md rounded-3xl shadow-[0_12px_28px_rgba(15,23,42,0.08)] border-2 border-primary-container/40 text-center space-y-2">
<div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-primary-fixed text-on-primary-fixed text-label-sm font-extrabold">المحطة 2</span>
<h3 class="font-headline-md text-headline-sm md:text-headline-md text-on-surface font-extrabold">تتبع الكلمات بالريشة الذكية ✍️</h3>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant max-w-xs mx-auto">
              تدرّبي على كتابة حرف "الشين" و"السين" بحركات ممتعة وأصوات تفاعلية مرحة!
            </p>
<div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-space-md text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant py-1">
<span class="flex items-center gap-1 font-bold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-base">timer</span>
                متبقي 5 دقائق
              </span>
<span>•</span>
<span class="flex items-center gap-1 font-bold text-primary">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-base">redeem</span>
                مكافأة: 40 XP
              </span>
</div>
<!-- Chunky 3D Duolingo-style Action Button -->
<button class="w-full max-w-xs mx-auto py-3 px-space-lg rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary-container font-headline-sm text-body-lg font-black shadow-[0_6px_0_#b45309,0_12px_20px_rgba(245,158,11,0.35)] hover:brightness-105 active:translate-y-1 active:shadow-[0_2px_0_#b45309] transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2" onclick="startMissionAlert()">
<span>ابدأ المغامرة الآن</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl">rocket_launch</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Milestone 3: LOCKED (Shifted Left) -->

<script>
    function toggleAudioSpeech() {
      const btn = document.getElementById('audioPromptBtn');
      const text = document.getElementById('audioText');
      const icon = document.getElementById('audioIcon');
      
      if (text.innerText.includes('استمع')) {
        text.innerText = 'سنبل يتحدث الآن... 🎵';
        icon.innerText = 'graphic_eq';
        btn.classList.add('animate-pulse', 'bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
        
        // Simple synthetic speech cue
        if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
          const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance('أهلاً بك يا بطلة سارة! جاهزة لمغامرة اليوم الشيقة؟ تتبعي حرف الشين معي الآن');
          utterance.lang = 'ar-SA';
          utterance.onend = function() {
            resetAudioBtn();
          };
          window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
        } else {
          setTimeout(resetAudioBtn, 3500);
        }
      } else {
        if ('speechSynthesis' in window) window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
        resetAudioBtn();
      }
    }

    function resetAudioBtn() {
      const btn = document.getElementById('audioPromptBtn');
      const text = document.getElementById('audioText');
      const icon = document.getElementById('audioIcon');
      text.innerText = '🔊 استمع لرسالة سنبل';
      icon.innerText = 'volume_up';
      btn.classList.remove('animate-pulse', 'bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
    }

    function startMissionAlert() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('interactiveModal');
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeMissionAlert() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('interactiveModal');
      modal.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  </script>

<script>
  // Simple client-side category filter for activities
  (function() {
    const filterButtons = document.querySelectorAll('#activity-filters .filter-btn');
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('#activities-cards-container .activity-card');

    filterButtons.forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        // Toggle active button style
        filterButtons.forEach(b => {
          b.classList.remove('bg-primary-container', 'text-on-primary-container', 'shadow-[0_4px_0_#b45309]');
          b.classList.add('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
          const dot = b.querySelector('span.w-2');
          if (dot) dot.remove();
        });

        btn.classList.remove('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
        btn.classList.add('bg-primary-container', 'text-on-primary-container', 'shadow-[0_4px_0_#b45309]');

        const activeCategory = btn.getAttribute('data-filter');

        cards.forEach(card => {
          const cardCat = card.getAttribute('data-category');
          if (activeCategory === 'all' || cardCat === activeCategory) {
            card.style.display = 'flex';
            card.classList.add('animate-fadeIn');
          } else {
            card.style.display = 'none';
          }
        });
      });
    });
  })();
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-40 md:hidden bg-surface-container-lowest/95 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-4px_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)] py-space-xs px-space-md flex items-center justify-around" data-active-classes="bg-primary-container text-on-primary-container font-extrabold shadow-md"><a class="flex flex-col items-center py-1 px-space-sm rounded-lg text-on-surface-variant text-label-sm font-bold" data-path="student-dashboard" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl">map</span><span>الخريطة</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center py-1 px-space-sm rounded-lg bg-primary-container text-on-primary-container font-extrabold shadow-md" data-path="student-activities" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl">sports_esports</span><span>الأنشطة</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center py-1 px-space-sm rounded-lg text-on-surface-variant text-label-sm font-bold" data-path="student-progress" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl">trending_up</span><span>تقدمي</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center py-1 px-space-sm rounded-lg text-on-surface-variant text-label-sm font-bold" data-path="student-badges" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl">military_tech</span><span>جوائزي</span></a></nav><footer class="w-full bg-surface-container-lowest py-space-lg border-t border-surface-container-low hidden md:block"><div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-gutter flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-space-md text-on-surface-variant text-body-sm font-body-sm"><span>🌱 سنابل IQ - رحلة تعليمية ذكية وتفاعلية ممتعة للأطفال</span><div class="flex items-center gap-space-lg"><a class="hover:text-on-surface transition-colors font-bold" data-path="parent-portal" href="#">إرشادات أولياء الأمور</a><a class="hover:text-on-surface transition-colors font-bold" data-path="help-center" href="#">مركز المساعدة والمرح</a></div></div></footer></body></html>

<script>
  // Simple interactive micro-interactions for child motivation
  const cheerBtn = document.getElementById('cheerBtn');
  if (cheerBtn) {
    cheerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      cheerBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-xl animate-spin">favorite</span><span>سنبل يحبك جداً! ✨</span>';
      setTimeout(() => {
        cheerBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-xl">celebration</span><span>شجّع سُنبُل الآن!</span>';
      }, 2500);
    });
  }

  const playAudioBtn = document.getElementById('playAudioBtn');
  const playIcon = document.getElementById('playIcon');
  const audioStatusText = document.getElementById('audioStatusText');
  let isPlaying = false;

  if (playAudioBtn && playIcon && audioStatusText) {
    playAudioBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      isPlaying = !isPlaying;
      if (isPlaying) {
        playIcon.textContent = 'pause';
        audioStatusText.textContent = 'جاري الاستماع: "أحسنتِ يا بطلة سارة، نطق حرف النون مذهل!" 🌟';
        setTimeout(() => {
          isPlaying = false;
          playIcon.textContent = 'volume_up';
          audioStatusText.textContent = 'اضغطي للاستماع إلى تشجيع سنبل الصوتي';
        }, 3500);
      } else {
        playIcon.textContent = 'volume_up';
        audioStatusText.textContent = 'اضغطي للاستماع إلى تشجيع سنبل الصوتي';
      }
    });
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-40 md:hidden bg-surface-container-lowest/95 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-4px_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)] py-space-xs px-space-md flex items-center justify-around" data-active-classes="bg-primary-container text-on-primary-container font-extrabold shadow-md"><a class="flex flex-col items-center py-1 px-space-sm rounded-lg text-on-surface-variant text-label-sm font-bold" data-path="student-dashboard" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl">map</span><span>الخريطة</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center py-1 px-space-sm rounded-lg text-on-surface-variant text-label-sm font-bold" data-path="student-activities" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl">sports_esports</span><span>الأنشطة</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center py-1 px-space-sm rounded-lg bg-primary-container text-on-primary-container font-extrabold shadow-md" data-path="student-progress" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl">trending_up</span><span>تقدمي</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center py-1 px-space-sm rounded-lg text-on-surface-variant text-label-sm font-bold" data-path="student-badges" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl">military_tech</span><span>جوائزي</span></a></nav><footer class="w-full bg-surface-container-lowest py-space-lg border-t border-surface-container-low hidden md:block"><div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-gutter flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-space-md text-on-surface-variant text-body-sm font-body-sm"><span>🌱 سنابل IQ - رحلة تعليمية ذكية وتفاعلية ممتعة للأطفال</span><div class="flex items-center gap-space-lg"><a class="hover:text-on-surface transition-colors font-bold" data-path="parent-portal" href="#">إرشادات أولياء الأمور</a><a class="hover:text-on-surface transition-colors font-bold" data-path="help-center" href="#">مركز المساعدة والمرح</a></div></div></footer></body></html>

<!-- Interactive Audio SFX Simulation and Certificate Modal Container -->
<div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 hidden bg-inverse-surface/40 backdrop-blur-sm items-center justify-center p-space-md transition-opacity duration-300" id="cert-modal">

<div class="text-right">

</div>

<!-- Inline Micro-Interactions Script -->
<script>
    function filterBadges(category, btn) {
      // Update Tab Styles
      const buttons = document.querySelectorAll('.badge-filter');
      buttons.forEach(b => {
        b.classList.remove('bg-primary-container', 'text-on-primary-container', 'font-extrabold', 'shadow-sm');
        b.classList.add('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface-variant', 'font-bold');
      });
      btn.classList.add('bg-primary-container', 'text-on-primary-container', 'font-extrabold', 'shadow-sm');
      btn.classList.remove('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface-variant', 'font-bold');

      // Filter Badge Cards
      const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.badge-card');
      const trophySection = document.getElementById('trophies-section');

      if (category === 'trophies') {
        trophySection.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        cards.forEach(card => {
          if (card.dataset.category === 'trophies') {
            card.style.display = 'flex';
          } else {
            card.style.display = 'none';
          }
        });
        return;
      }

      trophySection.style.display = 'block';

      cards.forEach(card => {
        if (category === 'all' || card.dataset.category === category) {
          card.style.display = 'flex';
        } else {
          card.style.display = 'none';
        }
      });
    }

    function openCertificate(title, desc) {
      document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = 'شهادة ' + title;
      document.getElementById('modal-desc').innerText = desc;
      const modal = document.getElementById('cert-modal');
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
      modal.classList.add('flex');
    }

    function closeCertificate() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('cert-modal');
      modal.classList.add('hidden');
      modal.classList.remove('flex');
    }

    function printOrSaveCert() {
      alert('🌟 تم حفظ شهادة الإنجاز بنجاح في ملفك الشخصي كصورة فائقة الجودة!');
      closeCertificate();
    }

    function shareAchievements() {
      if (navigator.share) {
        navigator.share({
          title: 'إنجازات سارة في سنابل IQ',
          text: 'شاهدوا أوسمتي وكؤوسي البراقة في سنابل IQ! حصلت على 6 أوسمة و 3 كؤوس ذهبية وفضية!',
          url: window.location.href,
        }).catch(() => {});
      } else {
        alert('🎉 تم نسخ رابط خزانة إنجازاتك لمشاركته مع المعلمة والعائلة!');
      }
    }
  </script>
